  - **DISCIPLINE-HARDENING-CONVENTION-1** The three patterns are each defined with a shape and a worked example
    - test: `tests/atomic/discipline/verification-before-completion.test.ts` › [DISCIPLINE-HARDENING-CONVENTION-1] gives each pattern a required form and the failure mode it prevents
  - **DISCIPLINE-HARDENING-CONVENTION-2** The rationalization-table headers are pinned
    - test: `tests/atomic/discipline/verification-before-completion.test.ts` › [DISCIPLINE-HARDENING-CONVENTION-2] pins the two-column Thought/Excuse → Reality form
  - **DISCIPLINE-HARDENING-CONVENTION-3** The HARD-GATE carries the too-simple anti-pattern
    - test: `tests/atomic/discipline/verification-before-completion.test.ts` › [DISCIPLINE-HARDENING-CONVENTION-3] requires the "too simple to need the gate" paragraph
    - test: `tests/atomic/discipline/verification-before-completion.test.ts` › [DISCIPLINE-HARDENING-CONVENTION-3] explains why the gate fails without it
  - **DISCIPLINE-HARDENING-CONVENTION-4** It is not a skill, and is not installed
    - test: `tests/atomic/discipline/verification-before-completion.test.ts` › [DISCIPLINE-HARDENING-CONVENTION-4] skills/_conventions/ contains no SKILL.md
  - **DISCIPLINE-HARDENING-CONVENTION-5** A directory without a SKILL.md is never treated as a bundle
    - test: `tests/spec/discipline/packaging.test.ts` › [DISCIPLINE-HARDENING-CONVENTION-5] every installed directory is a real bundle — each carries a SKILL.md
    - test: `tests/spec/discipline/packaging.test.ts` › [DISCIPLINE-HARDENING-CONVENTION-5] listSkillBundles is the shared enumeration, and it excludes SKILL.md-less dirs
  - **DISCIPLINE-HARDENING-CONVENTION-6** A skill installed at 4.0 carries no pointer to the convention file
    - test: `tests/atomic/discipline/verification-before-completion.test.ts` › [DISCIPLINE-HARDENING-CONVENTION-6] points at no repo-side file, because it is installed at 4.0
  - **DISCIPLINE-HARDENING-CONVENTION-7** The convention states the authoring test that keeps strawmen out of the table
    - test: `tests/atomic/discipline/verification-before-completion.test.ts` › [DISCIPLINE-HARDENING-CONVENTION-7] states the authoring test that keeps strawmen out
  - **DISCIPLINE-HARDENING-CONVENTION-8** A hedged Iron Law is forbidden
    - test: `tests/atomic/discipline/verification-before-completion.test.ts` › [DISCIPLINE-HARDENING-CONVENTION-8] forbids the hedged Iron Law
  - **DISCIPLINE-HARDENING-CONVENTION-9** The convention warns against reflowing grafted lines across line breaks
    - test: `tests/atomic/discipline/verification-before-completion.test.ts` › [DISCIPLINE-HARDENING-CONVENTION-9] warns against reflowing grafted lines across line breaks
- spec **discipline.verification-skill** (`specflow/impl/discipline/verification-skill.spec.md`)
  - **DISCIPLINE-VERIFICATION-SKILL-1** The Iron Law and its letter-and-spirit clause are present
    - test: `tests/atomic/discipline/verification-before-completion.test.ts` › [DISCIPLINE-VERIFICATION-SKILL-1] carries the Iron Law verbatim on one line
    - test: `tests/atomic/discipline/verification-before-completion.test.ts` › [DISCIPLINE-VERIFICATION-SKILL-1] follows the law with the letter-and-spirit clause
  - **DISCIPLINE-VERIFICATION-SKILL-2** The gate requires fresh evidence before a "tests pass" claim
    - test: `tests/atomic/discipline/verification-before-completion.test.ts` › [DISCIPLINE-VERIFICATION-SKILL-2] requires the test command to be run and cited for a "tests pass" claim
  - **DISCIPLINE-VERIFICATION-SKILL-3** The claim→evidence table covers the named claim kinds
    - test: `tests/atomic/discipline/verification-before-completion.test.ts` › [DISCIPLINE-VERIFICATION-SKILL-3] has a two-column claim → evidence table
    - test: `tests/atomic/discipline/verification-before-completion.test.ts` › [DISCIPLINE-VERIFICATION-SKILL-3] names cortex validate as the evidence for a schema/spec claim
  - **DISCIPLINE-VERIFICATION-SKILL-4** The rationalization table answers the pressure excuses
    - test: `tests/atomic/discipline/verification-before-completion.test.ts` › [DISCIPLINE-VERIFICATION-SKILL-4] uses the pinned two-column header form
    - test: `tests/atomic/discipline/verification-before-completion.test.ts` › [DISCIPLINE-VERIFICATION-SKILL-4] has at least five rationalization rows
  - **DISCIPLINE-VERIFICATION-SKILL-5** An unverifiable claim is narrowed, not skipped
    - test: `tests/atomic/discipline/verification-before-completion.test.ts` › [DISCIPLINE-VERIFICATION-SKILL-5] directs the agent to narrow the claim and state what was not verified
    - test: `tests/atomic/discipline/verification-before-completion.test.ts` › [DISCIPLINE-VERIFICATION-SKILL-5] forbids making the unqualified claim anyway
  - **DISCIPLINE-VERIFICATION-SKILL-6** The skill runs without a spec tree
    - test: `tests/atomic/discipline/verification-before-completion.test.ts` › [DISCIPLINE-VERIFICATION-SKILL-6] states that it requires no spec tree and no Cortex module
    - test: `tests/atomic/discipline/verification-before-completion.test.ts` › [DISCIPLINE-VERIFICATION-SKILL-6] never makes a step conditional on .specflow/ existing
  - **DISCIPLINE-VERIFICATION-SKILL-7** Package and local copies are identical · unverified
  - **DISCIPLINE-VERIFICATION-SKILL-8** The Iron Law opens the body, before the workflow
    - test: `tests/atomic/discipline/verification-before-completion.test.ts` › [DISCIPLINE-VERIFICATION-SKILL-8] states the law before the workflow, not buried in an appendix
  - **DISCIPLINE-VERIFICATION-SKILL-9** Fresh evidence means produced this turn, after the last change
    - test: `tests/atomic/discipline/verification-before-completion.test.ts` › [DISCIPLINE-VERIFICATION-SKILL-9] defines freshness as this turn, after the last change
  - **DISCIPLINE-VERIFICATION-SKILL-10** The gate is a numbered procedure ending in obtain-or-narrow
    - test: `tests/atomic/discipline/verification-before-completion.test.ts` › [DISCIPLINE-VERIFICATION-SKILL-10] spells out the gate as a numbered procedure ending in obtain-or-narrow
  - **DISCIPLINE-VERIFICATION-SKILL-11** The table gives a generalisation rule for unlisted claims
    - test: `tests/atomic/discipline/verification-before-completion.test.ts` › [DISCIPLINE-VERIFICATION-SKILL-11] gives the generalisation rule for unlisted claims
  - **DISCIPLINE-VERIFICATION-SKILL-12** A Cortex-layer read is enrichment, never a precondition
    - test: `tests/atomic/discipline/verification-before-completion.test.ts` › [DISCIPLINE-VERIFICATION-SKILL-12] treats any Cortex-layer read as enrichment, never a precondition
  - **DISCIPLINE-VERIFICATION-SKILL-13** The body carries a worked example of the gate firing
    - test: `tests/atomic/discipline/verification-before-completion.test.ts` › [DISCIPLINE-VERIFICATION-SKILL-13] shows the mechanism firing on a realistic case

  - **HOOKS-PRE-WRITE-3** No matching rule → empty stdout

  - **HOOKS-PRE-WRITE-6** Predicate passes on a governed path → silent (B-001 regression)

    - test: `tests/spec/hooks/pre-write.test.ts` › [HOOKS-PRE-WRITE-6] R-001 with an expect:absent regex, Write to a governed path with conforming content → exit 0, empty stdout

    - test: `tests/spec/hooks/pre-write.test.ts` › [HOOKS-PRE-WRITE-7] R-030 with governs and no check: field → Write to src/db/schema.ts warns naming R-030
  - **HOOKS-PRE-WRITE-8** Retired rules are silent

    - test: `tests/spec/hooks/pre-write.test.ts` › [HOOKS-PRE-WRITE-8] path-matching rule with status: retired → empty stdout

  - **HOOKS-SESSION-START-1** Fresh hygiene report → pointer plus one-line summary

- spec **hooks.session-end** (`specflow/impl/hooks/session-end.spec.md`)
  - **HOOKS-SESSION-END-1** Registered under SessionEnd and Stop by init and sync
    - test: `tests/atomic/core-cli/init.test.ts` › [HOOKS-SESSION-END-1] registers SessionEnd (timeout: 10, no matcher) and Stop (no timeout, no matcher) exactly once each
    - test: `tests/atomic/core-cli/sync.test.ts` › [HOOKS-SESSION-END-1] a project initialised before the SessionEnd/Stop rows gains both on sync, once, with timeout on SessionEnd only; a second sync is idempotent
    - test: `tests/atomic/schema/hook-config.test.ts` › [HOOKS-SESSION-END-1] both present alongside the other Cortex entries → passes; an edited timeout keeps the file conformant
  - **HOOKS-SESSION-END-2** The Stop companion records the last assistant message and nothing else
    - test: `tests/atomic/hooks/stop.test.ts` › [HOOKS-SESSION-END-2] writes text capped at exactly 2000 characters and an iso `at`; stdout empty; nothing else changes
  - **HOOKS-SESSION-END-3** The Stop companion is silent on missing input
    - test: `tests/atomic/hooks/stop.test.ts` › [HOOKS-SESSION-END-3] %s → exit 0, empty stdout, no file, no hook-errors.md
  - **HOOKS-SESSION-END-4** A question left hanging becomes the record's open question
    - test: `tests/atomic/hooks/session-end.test.ts` › [HOOKS-SESSION-END-4] the last assistant paragraph ending in ? with no user message after → question from the transcript
    - test: `tests/spec/hooks/session-end.test.ts` › [HOOKS-SESSION-END-4] writes the full §4.5.3 record with the question from the transcript; stdout empty, exit 0
  - **HOOKS-SESSION-END-5** A fresher companion file wins over a lagging transcript and is then deleted
    - test: `tests/atomic/hooks/session-end.test.ts` › [HOOKS-SESSION-END-5] companion `at` later than every tail timestamp → its final paragraph, source stop, timestamp at
    - test: `tests/spec/hooks/session-end.test.ts` › [HOOKS-SESSION-END-5] uses the companion's final paragraph with source stop and its `at`; the file is gone afterwards
    - test: `tests/spec/hooks/session-end.test.ts` › [HOOKS-SESSION-END-5] the companion file written by `cortex hook stop` is the one SessionEnd consumes
  - **HOOKS-SESSION-END-6** An older companion file is ignored and still deleted
    - test: `tests/atomic/hooks/session-end.test.ts` › [HOOKS-SESSION-END-6] companion `at` not later than the tail → ignored; the transcript rule decides
    - test: `tests/spec/hooks/session-end.test.ts` › [HOOKS-SESSION-END-6] companion at 09:58 vs a transcript ending with a user message at 10:00 → open_question null, file deleted
  - **HOOKS-SESSION-END-7** An offer is recorded as an offer, an answered question is not recorded
    - test: `tests/atomic/hooks/session-end.test.ts` › [HOOKS-SESSION-END-7] a user message after the last assistant text → null; a plain statement → null
    - test: `tests/atomic/hooks/session-end.test.ts` › [HOOKS-SESSION-END-7] the offer lexicon yields kind offer; text is capped at 600 characters
    - test: `tests/spec/hooks/session-end.test.ts` › [HOOKS-SESSION-END-7] offer with nothing after → kind offer; the same followed by a user reply → null
  - **HOOKS-SESSION-END-8** Approvals are paired with what was approved
    - test: `tests/atomic/hooks/session-end.test.ts` › [HOOKS-SESSION-END-8] pairs a lexicon-matching user message with the preceding assistant paragraph; skips one with no assistant before
    - test: `tests/atomic/hooks/session-end.test.ts` › [HOOKS-SESSION-END-8] a match with no preceding assistant message at all is skipped; non-matching user text is ignored
    - test: `tests/spec/hooks/session-end.test.ts` › [HOOKS-SESSION-END-8] records one entry pairing the approval with the preceding assistant paragraph
    - test: `tests/spec/hooks/session-end.test.ts` › [HOOKS-SESSION-END-8] a `proceed` with no assistant message before it in the transcript is skipped
  - **HOOKS-SESSION-END-9** Harness-injected user entries are not approvals and do not answer questions
    - test: `tests/atomic/hooks/session-end.test.ts` › [HOOKS-SESSION-END-9] humanMessages keeps order and drops only the injected user entries
    - test: `tests/spec/hooks/session-end.test.ts` › [HOOKS-SESSION-END-9] teammate, system-reminder and tool_result-only user entries are ignored; the human's Approved. counts and replies
    - test: `tests/spec/hooks/session-end.test.ts` › [HOOKS-SESSION-END-9] a question followed only by a <task-notification> user entry is still the open question
  - **HOOKS-SESSION-END-10** Tagged findings are captured, malformed tags are skipped

  - **HOOKS-PROMPT-ROUTE-1** Registered under UserPromptSubmit by init and sync, required by the validator
    - test: `tests/atomic/core-cli/init.test.ts` › [HOOKS-PROMPT-ROUTE-1] registers the UserPromptSubmit prompt-route row once, with no matcher and no timeout, regardless of hooks.preRead (hooks.prompt-route Rule 1)
    - test: `tests/atomic/core-cli/sync.test.ts` › [HOOKS-PROMPT-ROUTE-1] a project initialised before the UserPromptSubmit row gains `cortex hook prompt-route` on sync, once, with no matcher and the other events untouched; a second sync is idempotent
    - test: `tests/atomic/schema/hook-config.test.ts` › [HOOKS-PROMPT-ROUTE-1] other Cortex entries present but the row missing → exactly one error keyed hooks.UserPromptSubmit naming `cortex hook prompt-route`, `UserPromptSubmit` and `cortex sync`
    - test: `tests/atomic/schema/hook-config.test.ts` › [HOOKS-PROMPT-ROUTE-1] is not behind hooks.preRead (nor hooks.readDefer): with the flag false and no Read pair, the row is still required — and its presence passes
    - test: `tests/spec/hooks/prompt-route.test.ts` › [HOOKS-PROMPT-ROUTE-1] init registers exactly one UserPromptSubmit entry — `cortex hook prompt-route`, no matcher — alongside the other eight, and the project validates clean on check.hook-config
    - test: `tests/spec/hooks/prompt-route.test.ts` › [HOOKS-PROMPT-ROUTE-1] a settings file carrying `cortex hook session-start` but no prompt-route entry → one check.hook-config error naming `cortex hook prompt-route` and `cortex sync`
  - **HOOKS-PROMPT-ROUTE-2** The first prompt of a session surfaces the previous session's hanging question

    - test: `tests/spec/hooks/prompt-route.test.ts` › [HOOKS-PROMPT-ROUTE-2] first prompt of s2 → the Open: line for the thread through the real dispatcher; the fired memory records it; the thread file is untouched
  - **HOOKS-PROMPT-ROUTE-3** Resumption has four gates and each one holds

    - test: `tests/atomic/hooks/prompt-route.test.ts` › [HOOKS-PROMPT-ROUTE-3] (e) s1.json's session_id is s2 (this session's own record) → skipped → silent

    - test: `tests/atomic/hooks/prompt-route.test.ts` › [HOOKS-PROMPT-ROUTE-4] the lowercase `t-003` is not a ref → nothing

    - test: `tests/atomic/recall/query.test.ts` › [HOOKS-PROMPT-ROUTE-10] when even one 20-char line with the tail is over budget, the tail goes last

    - test: `tests/spec/schema/bears-on.spec.test.ts` › [SCHEMA-BEARS-ON-6] each of the two files carries a check.bears-on error at key bears_on
